- **clarify-raw-captures**: Save and clarify Raw/Captures notes without replacing the user's thinking. Use for `Capture：`, `仅保存 Capture：`, fragments, questions, observations, hypotheses, voice-to-text notes, unfiltered AI responses, or loose conversation excerpts. Preserve the original record, confirm the original context before understanding previews or semantic searches, and do not create mature Wiki conclusions by default. Use for personal experiences, project retrospectives, decision processes, state observations, practice results, conversation digests, or already compiled Experiences. ([seegerzelle-creator/second-brain-starter](https://github.com/seegerzelle-creator/second-brain-starter/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/clarify-raw-experiences))
